## 2026-11-16 — Phone v2.3.125 watchlist refresh failure and app-only fix

- **User phone:** Installed v2.3.125; Watchlist displayed last-saved RELIANCE ₹1339.70 but user did not observe the expected first-login quote refresh. This is a device FAIL report; do not imply the last saved number must change during a market closure.
- **Oracle evidence from preceding checks:** OAuth authenticated, 51 equity snapshot, and S19 3 index quotes returned HTTP 200. No new Oracle requests or production changes needed for this source fix.
- **Source root-cause candidates confirmed in code:**
  1. Closed-session retry in 2.3.125 stopped when it saw *any old saved quote*, instead of proving a full successful snapshot for the *current login provider generation*. On a failed initial fetch it could strand the Watchlist until market open.
  2. Login snapshot outcome updated `market_status` but did not set visible `watch_batch_status`, leaving Watchlist status stale/inconclusive.
- **Changed:** `5da68b3a` adds current-generation equity success gate and bounded closed-session recovery; `9d94169a` adds expression-based behavior tests and a compact Watchlist quote status; `bd5c2212` routes login success/partial/failure to Watchlist status and last-success attempt clock.
- **CI:** `bd5c2212` offline regression 809/809 PASS:
  https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/43753705425.
- **Next candidate:** 2.3.126 signed release; verify build, signer, versionCode and artifact separately, then update from installed 2.3.125 **without clearing app storage**.
- **Pending mobile proof:** connect/check Dhan; verify 'Login snapshot' status on Watchlist, open/closed display, index batch, Super Five, rate-cadence at 09:15–15:30 IST and failure/retry. Last-known/unverified must not be described as a live exchange quote.
- **Oracle:** unchanged; watchlist server S18 cross-list restriction remains a separate staged deployment gate. Dhan OAuth and orders unchanged.

### E. Agreed rules / do not break in future patches

- First authenticated login (open OR closed): one bounded read-only load of
  latest available equity snapshot, independently loaded 3-index batch, and
  available saved candle/ranking/history for Dashboard/Watchlist/Super Five.
  A previously saved quote is NOT proof that current login fetched a snapshot.
- Closed market: after initial attempt and bounded recovery, pause automatic
  repetitive quote and history requests; still show last-known Dhan prices
  labeled **NOT LIVE / exchange timestamp unverified**.
- Normal session: existing `regular_hours()` checks Monday–Friday 09:15 to
  15:30 IST; Watchlist/equity snapshot and Index poll each independently
  rate-limited (currently 60s); separate candle scan roughly 180s; fast local
  Scanner/Super Five cache refresh paths must stay independent. Do not silently
  change these intervals. Official NSE/BSE holidays/special sessions not
  fully modeled: current weekday/time check alone is not authoritative.
- Preserve valid last-known quote on failed/partial Dhan response. 429
  cooldown, request serialization, bounded selected symbols and no repeated
  51-history fanout.
- Exactly **five** Watchlists with **70 different equities total** and a
  symbol in **one** list only; same rule server-side once S18 deployed.
- Dhan-only market-data provenance, no silent Angel/Yahoo fallback.
  Per-engine News/Futures/Options/Candle percentages need independent sourced,
  symbol-matched, timestamp-checked inputs. Missing data = WAIT, no fabricated
  directional signal. Historical candles must not be labeled verified live.
- App version label visible; signed upgrade must preserve Android package,
  signing identity, old watchlists, paper profiles and local saved settings.
- **PAPER trades only**; live broker order placement OFF. Keep token renewal
  unchanged; no credential exposure or unauthorized live orders.

13. **Final release gate:** full offline/backend CI, signed APK artifact

## 2026-11-16 — Batch 127-D option-chain cooldown + stale-selection repair

- **Source audit:** Existing option polling attempted every >=60s during market hours, but the Dhan gateway deliberately caches option HTTP 422 invalid underlying/expiry responses for 300s and applies 429 provider-wide cooldown. Repeated scheduled attempts can overwrite option diagnostics and create avoidable local retries even when the upstream is guarded. This is a **source-level defect**, not a current authenticated provider observation.
- **[97134aa7](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/97134aa7c363ab1bbef7f8307d307e905c1e0d89):** adds bounded (32), in-memory `option_retry_until` per provider generation, symbol, selection mode and manual expiry; 422 waits 300s; 429 honors at least 30s and Dhan gateway cooldown; successful chosen chain clears its key. Scheduled polling and manual actions guard both Dhan option endpoints; saved CE/PE remains unverified and no broker orders are introduced.
- **[7804c9d1](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/7804c9d168771ef786a6473b64af87b6e613683e):** adds 127-D source contract regression for bounded cooldown and per-symbol/expiry isolation.

## 2026-11-16 — Joint SERVER ↔ DHAN ports, pairing key, token, quote quota audit

**Scope / external rulebook:** DhanHQ v2 official authentication, Market Quote and API rate-limit documentation verified on 2026-11-16:
- Market Quote `POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/ltp` requires `access-token` and `client-id` headers, up to 1000 instruments per call and **1 quote request per second**; see https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/market-quote/ .
- Dhan API token validity is normally **24h**; OAuth consent API key/secret and the 24h access token have different validity. Dhan profile returns actual `dataPlan`, `dataValidity`, active segments and token validity; configured credentials do not establish entitlement; see https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/authentication/ .
- DhanHQ v2 API rate caps distinguish Quote (1/sec) from historical Data (5/sec, up to 100k/day). Option Chain uses separate 3-second pacing and listed expiries; see https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/ and /releases/ . Broker trading/order endpoints remain intentionally DISABLED in BOT KING.

**Source-level connection model:** external Android ↔ owner domain HTTPS 443 (Nginx) ↔ localhost 127.0.0.1:8000 (Uvicorn FastAPI). `X-BotKing-Key` is server pairing/auth only, **never the Dhan access token**. Oracle ↔ Dhan market/auth endpoints use TLS 443 and OAuth app credentials or protected read-only token. GitHub `backend/install-oracle.sh` declares /dhan/ and /v2/ reverse proxies; `backend/server.py` enforces 403 on absent/wrong pairing and no live orders. **Real Oracle firewall/Nginx/systemd, route deployment, env/session file mode, outbound DNS/TLS, Dhan token validity and dataPlan remain UNVERIFIED:** GitHub source ≠ deployed Oracle.

## 2026-11-16 — BK local stale-age UI update without API polling; new targeted regression

- **Functional correctness audit:** The new 2s mounted-row observer previously compared only changed source quote/signals. A recent quote's on-screen freshness label could remain "LTP time unverified" beyond its 60s age threshold if no broker reply changed the quote; likewise scanner/five-engine state could age beyond its allowed window without triggering a UI cell repaint.

## 2026-11-16 — USER MOBILE BUG: red/green Watchlist candles disappear progressively, NIFTY LTP red but no candle, five filters WAIT — active repair

- **New user-observed device symptom (FAIL, overrides old green CI):** Watchlist candle B/S red-green first appears then disappears row-by-row and becomes neutral/white; NIFTY price/movement red while independent 5m Candle B/S graphic absent; several filter indicators read `WAIT`. Exact installed APK not established by this report; prior signed build 2.3.127 #642 PASS alone cannot prove mobile rendering.
- **Root-cause candidate from real source audit:** historical scan `refresh_market()->load_one(sym)` replaced `quotes[sym]` with `get_market_data(...,5m,2d)` even when incoming `bars=[]` or a short/stale window, causing last valid completed history to be lost; `shared_history(sym)` subsequently has no valid candles, while `refresh_shared_analysis()` may write a newer empty analysis on top of a valid cached red/green filter vote. A second contributing factor: `snapshot_for_display` intentionally expires engine eligibility at 300 seconds; technical HIST fallback had an unnecessary `dhan_feed.connected` requirement. Index LTP price movement is not itself proof of a 5m candle vote and must remain separately labeled.
- **Implemented source fixes:** [f79778ec](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/f79778ec3ef80839775c10ae7668b9a1049ed015) adds pure same-provider `retain_candle_history()` guarding previous valid 30+ bars, rejecting older/partial history and flagging retained candles as HIST/unverified; [9ad04599](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/9ad045994f8e04114fddbdb10ed85bcdcf053adc) integrates this before `cycle_data`/`quotes` replacement. [227cf84e](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/227cf84ecff31c54197410f982ee4ef5e250c523) adds 7 regressions for empty/partial/older/cross-source/invalid OHLC and newest valid series.
- **Additional non-fabrication:** [6316caa6](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/6316caa640ce9b3437ca52bdd3ad133f9080109c) adds `retain_last_filter_votes` requiring exact same selected-filter names, real positive timestamp and Dhan source; returns historical votes with `bias=WAIT`, `score=0`, `live_verified=False` on failed newer analysis, so the old B/S visualization survives but does not trade. [158dcd95](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/158dcd9545374d2c92f66bec1702f53ab30ba5c6) integrates it into shared analysis. [b3a638b1](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/b3a638b1bb50df5ae241011398fe922f37e5f822) adds source/filters/live-guard regression tests.
- **Five-engine/index HIST presentation:** [cfa621f3](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/cfa621f34f935992c3b7c910533fbc4b49d8db37) allows older same-source technical votes to display with explicit `T HIST B/S` even when Dhan disconnected or cached engine has expired; does **not** mark 3 other engines LIVE; Dashboard NIFTY index uses previous real Dhan 5m filter votes as `HIST` if current 5m bars unavailable, rather than deriving Candle from red index LTP. ## 2026-11-16 — APP↔ORACLE contract parity audit before v2.3.129 device acceptance (SOURCE ONLY)

- **User question / rule:** App and production Oracle Server must agree on identical authenticated routes, payload schema, pacing, cache age, time window, historical provenance and 429 behavior BEFORE declaring mobile data delivery healthy. Signed APK CI does not validate live Oracle production.
- **Inspected GitHub source:** `src/server_feed.py`, `backend/server.py`, `docs/BOT_KING_ORACLE_APP_CONTRACT_20261115.md`, refresh inventory. App allows protected `/v2/snapshot`, `/dhan/index-ltp-batch`, `/dhan/selected-ltp-batch`, `/dhan/ltp-batch`, `/dhan/intraday`, `/dhan/option-expiries`, `/dhan/option-chain` and `/v2/assessment/{symbol}`. These main first seven routes appear in `backend/server.py` source, with `X-BotKing-Key` server-pairing protection; source existence does NOT prove customized Oracle Production has those exact handlers or matching returned contract.
- **Critical server/app cadence gap identified in backend source:** FastAPI lifespan sets `BOTKING_226_INGEST_ENABLED` default `0`, opt-in `1`; snapshot ingestion interval `BOTKING_226_INGEST_SECONDS` default 90s, clamped **min 60s / max 900s**. `/v2/snapshot` is process-local, starts with `not_collected`, and is only fresh if `recently_collected`; client `ServerDhanReadOnly.batch_quotes` rejects nonrecent age/status (>120s); fallback `equity_quotes_with_bootstrap` uses one direct-through-Oracle `/dhan/ltp-batch` at most every **180s**. Future 15–30s upstream snapshot refresh targets are therefore **NOT compatible with current server source min-60s settings** without a tested coordinated backend change. Collector being disabled/missing **may** explain stale snapshots but is not proven on actual deployed Oracle.
- Backend source index batch `/dhan/index-ltp-batch` maps three indices; source selected equity validates 1..70; option chain/expiry routes require mapped entitlement; `/dhan/intraday` caches identical symbol/interval/date for 180s, and 5m candle freshness must not be inferred from LTP. Mobile quote/option/index network currently min60s; four logical lanes and Super Five 2s are app-side source planning, NOT an Oracle production 4-pipeline deployment.

## 2026-11-16 — AUDIT-08 localhost redirect-protection hardening
- **Code review issue:** `scripts/audit_oracle_collector_runtime_readonly.py::request_get` created an urllib opener disabling proxy usage but not automatic HTTP redirects. A 30x from a protected local GET could potentially forward its pairing header to a different origin or move the audit outside trusted loopback. This is a **source-level security exposure**, **not an observed Oracle production attack**.
- **Source fix:** [92c3597f](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/92c3597f81cfaef80caeed8bed22864b01cf6947) adds `NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler)`, returning `None` for all redirect requests. Default opener uses `ProxyHandler({})` and `NoRedirect()`. 30x are returned as HTTP status and never followed; no Dhan/quote/order calls.
- **Regression:** [55cffa26](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/55cffa2642b53c4eeae33c876b68bc421ceecf2e) asserts the redirect handler refuses an external-origin redirect and is installed for the protected audit. **CI PENDING** at commit; do not claim successful tests yet.
- **Scope:** GitHub audited script only, not Oracle deployed source. No production change, no APK or live orders. Real server collector/assessment and equity PCR entitlement still unverified.

- **Exact Audit-08 CI started:** #1581 [run 43839664916](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/43839664916), head `55cffa26`, was **IN PROGRESS** when checked. This source/test security fix is not yet CI PASS; previous #1580 953/953 applies to prior commit.

## 2026-11-16 — UNIFIED-54 ASSESSMENT CACHE GATE 01
- **Confirmed source defect:** `src/shared_assessment_ui.py::assessment_lines` checked only whether fetched timestamp was int/float and compared it using inequalities. `NaN` is a float but all comparisons against it are false, so a malformed cached assessment timestamp could bypass expiry and reach display processing. `True` was also accepted as numeric. This shared helper is used for equities and indices; not evidence that Oracle produced a bad timestamp.
- **Source fix** [5d72c7c4](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/5d72c7c44c2a21ea56647d5cfa5be8f9f0e39709) rejects non-finite or boolean fetched/now/max_age and invalid negative max age; keeps the existing fail-closed `WAIT • cached assessment expired`. **Regression** [b9fd95b4](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/b9fd95b40fb773d9ec69d0f39feb764b12d62880) tests RELIANCE, HDFCBANK and NIFTY/BANKNIFTY/SENSEX for nonfinite/boolean timestamps. This is shared-helper protection for all 54 when invoked, not proof 54 assessments have been fetched. **New CI PENDING** at commit time.
- Historical failure #1585 and superseding #1586 958/958 PASS, plus #1587 961/961 PASS, documented in preceding entries. Oracle production audit, real five-filter producer, equity PCR entitlement and live device 54/54 remain **UNVERIFIED**; no APK, orders or deployment.


- **UNIFIED-54 ASSESSMENT CACHE GATE 02 — source-level crash risk:** `src/shared_assessment_ui.py` constructed `{f.get("id"): f for f in filters}`. If a malformed server filter `id` is a list/dict, constructing the dict raises `TypeError: unhashable type`, risking watchlist/scanner rendering. This is a proven code path, not observed live Oracle payload.
- **Fix** [3f0b0356](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/3f0b03569dcde59735d9291e010cbcb1f3421352) validates exactly five filter rows and string IDs from the known set before constructing the map. Invalid entries fail closed as `WAIT • incomplete filters`.
- **Test** [51f67706](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/51f67706b24e3010dfb5df5a5404785555128f77) checks list/dict/None/integer IDs in representative equity + all 3 indices. New CI pending. No new provider fanout, live Oracle deployment, or APK.

## 2026-11-16 — DUAL-MOBILE DATA REQUEST AUDIT / SHARED QUOTE LANE 01
- **Owner's goal:** Two separately installed phones, one server, one Dhan account/token, bounded rate-aware up-to-date read-only data, no duplicate provider fanout. **Source confirmed:** `GET /v2/snapshot` serves 51-equity cached quotes with no direct market requests when fresh, but `POST /dhan/index-ltp-batch` and `POST /dhan/selected-ltp-batch` each called `DhanFeed.batch_ltp` per phone without a shared response cache; 180s candle cache only helps after completion and has a concurrent miss race. Mobile manual/auto throttle is device-local and does NOT stop two-phone requests.
- **New server helper** [46cc4e2d](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/46cc4e2d0e2c774bcba2de0e2342af048ce4f413): `backend/shared_quote_lane.py::SharedQuoteLane`, one process-wide serialized read-only quote lane, same-key 60s verified-success reuse, bounded max 24 entries, shared 30s cooldown on `DhanRateLimitError`, never caches malformed or failed responses. No background market polling or trading calls.
- **Route integration** [1672629f](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/1672629f635619da412c3eeb8939270d2265cd1a) joins three-index and selected-equity `batch_ltp` paths to **one in-process cache/lane**. Keys segregate index vs selected instrument set; sorted selected symbols allow sharing despite order differences; incorporate a one-way SHA256 token fingerprint to invalidate cache across token rotation without retaining plaintext token as key. HTTP response remains `freshness=unverified`, `live_orders_enabled=false`.
- **Offline regression** [7055db1b](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/7055db1b8d6803776b9c3a8bf6bb497ed87fb9d2) asserts concurrent two callers trigger one provider fetch, distinct selected sets and token rotation cause new fetch, invalid response never cached, second phone respects 429 cooldown, TTL/size bound and routes are wired. **New CI PENDING**, offline source only. No real Oracle deployment yet; no APK/device proof.
- **Unresolved:** Fixed `/dhan/ltp-batch` initial recovery can still receive a separate request per phone if no snapshot; 180s candle cache may double-miss concurrently, plus option-chain/assessment must be reviewed. Current one-Uvicorn-worker server may use in-process lane; multiple workers/hosts would need distributed cache or lock. Do not equate 60s cache age with exchange freshness. Do not enable live orders or expand broker fanout.

## 2026-11-16 — SINGLE-MOBILE FIRST / DASHBOARD INDEX VOTE / SDC-4 WORK START

- **Owner's release decision:** Focus on ONE current BOT KING Android mobile. Defer two-phone onboarding, paired login and dual-device acceptance/deployment. Retain existing GitHub dual-client source work, but do not deploy it solely for two phones. **APK LAST**: no new signed APK until grouped code, exact-head CI, Oracle contract/runtime and device smoke gates are satisfied.
- **Current user-observed MOBILE FAIL:** The red/green Bullish/Bearish horizontal vote indicator is visible on Watchlist, but missing from all three top Dashboard cards: NIFTY / BANKNIFTY / SENSEX. This observation is separate from LTP presence, source CI or any exchange-live claim. Installed version of this phone at this report was NOT supplied; confirm before attributing to a specific APK.
- **Source audit:** `src/main.py::dashboard_market_indices` used `historical_vote_bar(historical_vote_agreement(shared_history(index_name)))`. It only fell back to `watch_signal_cache` when `index_history.get("bars")` was empty, not when bars were nonempty but completed/quality-filtered votes were unavailable. Thus partial/poor candle history could mask a valid previous same-source vote agreement and replace red/green with WAIT. This is a *confirmed source logic gap*, not proof that it is the only runtime cause across all three indices; missing both history and previous votes still produces WAIT correctly.
- **Source fix:** [050cc5ad](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/050cc5adc5216dd436fc0cbb23142b0a702dfffe) computes `index_agreement` and uses the previous cached Dhan vote whenever it is None, regardless of whether current bars are empty. Cached result explicitly renders `vote_display(counts,False)` = HIST / NOT live; never fabricates a fresh signal, never turns index LTP direction into candle voting, no new broker calls.
- **Regression:** [1ea0d0cc](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/1ea0d0cc942ae576d8694715cc8ffe728a53006b) adds `tests/test_dashboard_index_vote_retention_contract.py`: three-index common path, nonempty/unusable fallback and historical-only cached rendering. Exact-head CI: **PENDING VERIFICATION** at this journal entry. Mobile three-index bars: **PENDING RETEST**; no APK built. If no valid cached votes or complete bars exist, a truthful unavailable label is required, not a forced bar.
- **NEW DESIGN BK-SDC4-01 (PLANNED, NOT IMPLEMENTED):** Create a Smart Data Controller with dynamically calculated active-symbol subscriptions and four logical read-only lanes (Price/Indices; Options/PCR; Candle/Scanner/Assessment; News/Futures). Current 51 equities + 3 indices = 54; removing 20 equities yields 31 equities + 3 indices = 34; 70-equity watchlist cap remains separate from 3 indices. Remove polling for unused symbols *only if* they are not needed by selected Scanner, Super Five, Dashboard or a visible options panel. Do not assume 20 symbols = 20 upstream API requests saved; record actual batch sizes, serialized calls, byte counts, source age, 429s and historical-request counts.
- **SDC-4 control policy:** Priority-weighted fair queues, bounded per-endpoint Dhan request rate, server one-process shared cache/single-flight and cooldown, no starvation, visible per-lane pending/last-success/age/bytes/errors; increase suitable lane capacity only from real measured savings and never violate endpoint-specific caps. Current app quote/index lower-bound 60s, Candle and Assessment roughly 180s; collector nominal 90s with source default OFF (actual Oracle settings unverified). News live provider unverified. Re-evaluate only with real quota and end-to-end telemetry.

Super Five historically uses 5m; user described its current 5m then said Dashboard should 1m—separate dashboard embedded Super Five from the five-minute Watchlist ranking policy before changing it.

Scanner `scan_tf` selection already supports 1m/3m/5m processing separately, but verify selected-filter vote presentation on device. These are source facts, not phone validation.
- **Timeframe isolation safety:** Changing only the Dashboard caption from 5m to 1m would be false. Must fetch/retain real per-symbol **1m Dhan candle series** for Dashboard independently of Scanner's chosen timeframe; enforce source, symbol, generation, exact timeframe, completed-bar and quality checks with 60-second alignment. Do NOT reinterpret 5m candle votes as 1m or infer 1m OHLC by interpolation. Display `1m • HIST / NOT live` when only historical 1m values exist, and `1m data pending` rather than a fake red/green bar when no usable 1m bars are available.
- **Request-budget dependency:** Existing main automatic index-history work is **5m every ~180s**, while changing three indexes to separate auto-1m probes without quota planning would increase Dhan history fanout, risk HTTP 429 and harm Watchlist. Integrate 1m index candle input into planned BK-SDC4-01 single-mobile priority queue, share it with Scanner when same symbol/timeframe, and cap with Dhan endpoint budget. Dashboard render must never call Dhan itself.
- **Status:** POLICY AUDITED / CODE TIMEFRAME REFACTOR & ORACLE DATA VALIDATION **OPEN**. The earlier source fallback fix `050cc5ad` for nonempty unusable 5m index history is a separate historical continuity patch, not the new 1m feature; test `1ea0d0cc` CI/device still need proof. No APK or production change. ## 2026-11-16 — B3 read-only real request-flow SOURCE AUDIT, no functional patch
- **Baseline:** GitHub Actions [#1662](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/43883383158), commit `73580eae`, **1033/1033 offline PASS**. - **Observed actual mobile-to-Oracle request mechanics:** `ServerDhanReadOnly._call` holds `self._request_lock` around `_call_serialized`, serializing HTTPS calls **within a single client instance**. `_call_serialized` enforces shared `_global_cooldown_until` and per-path `_cooldown_until` on HTTP 429, respects `Retry-After` clamped to 1–120 seconds; caps read response at 2,000,000 bytes; accepts only read-only allowlisted routes and checks `live_orders_enabled is False`. The Android `get_market_data` caches matching symbol/timeframe/provider history for 90s; the independent index-history poll retries on 180s cadence, pauses when equity history scan active and uses 1.5s pacing for non-cache index requests.
- **Gap found:** `plan_due_history_symbols` is still unused by `src/main.py` (no call sites) and therefore its 3-symbol cap, per-symbol 180s and 1.5s global spacing DO NOT yet enforce actual broker traffic. The existing mobile-side lock/cooldown is per instance, NOT sufficient proof of shared Oracle quota control across two phones. Real traffic/byte budgets, Oracle upstream global serialization and two-mobile coordination remain **NOT VERIFIED**.
- **Decision:** Hold new app feature patches under user-requested slower workflow. Before wiring B3 planner, audit actual Oracle `/dhan/intraday` serial queue/429 policies with read-only source/metrics and design a server-owned shared cache/coordinator. ## 2026-11-16 — Follow-on job C: Oracle identical-history request cache and race audit
- Inspected the actual working branch `backend/server.py` `@app.post("/dhan/intraday")` handler after #1664 source CI PASS. The server **already has a 180-second validated history cache** keyed by `(body.symbol,str(body.interval))` with exact date-window matching and non-live provenance. This prevents serially repeated same-symbol/timeframe/date requests across phones on one worker from calling Dhan again. Earlier assumption that there was no backend shared cache is corrected by source evidence.
- **Remaining concrete race:** the handler checks `candle_cache` while holding `candle_lock`, then releases it before `DhanFeed(...).intraday(payload)` and re-locks later to write; two concurrent identical cache misses can therefore both initiate upstream requests before either cache result exists. New process-local 1.1s Dhan history lane [d192027e](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/d192027e69a7c4b4f8d27ec9e6882cd568d9b054) serializes/paces those upstream calls but does **not** coalesce concurrent misses.
- **Action held until evidence:** review the route's existing tests and design per-key server single-flight cache-miss protection (possibly bounded striped locks) without causing worker starvation. No extra functional patch committed during this follow-up source audit, no production restart or deployment, no multi-worker/distributed claims.

**Phase B, only proven targeted fix:** stage a diff and backup, add grouped regressions and negative safety tests; no duplicate API routes, auth bypass, fabricated bullish/bearish values, or live orders.
